*   [Complete coverage of Grand Prix Milan 2015](/en/articles/event-coverage/undated/grand-prix-milan-2015)

Due to travel circumstances, the structure for Grand Prix Milan 2015 differed from other Grand Prix. The Pro Point payouts were adjusted to accomodate the unique structure for this event.

**Grand Prix Milan (for Top 8 players)**

<table><tbody><tr><td>Top 8 Place</td>&#10;			<td>Pro Points</td>&#10;		</tr><tr><td>1</td>&#10;			<td>8</td>&#10;		</tr><tr><td>2</td>&#10;			<td>6</td>&#10;		</tr><tr><td>3-4</td>&#10;			<td>5</td>&#10;		</tr><tr><td>5-8</td>&#10;			<td>4</td>&#10;		</tr></tbody></table>

**Grand Prix Milan (for all other players)**

<table><tbody><tr><td>Day Two Match Points</td>&#10;			<td>Pro Points</td>&#10;		</tr><tr><td>18+</td>&#10;			<td>4*</td>&#10;		</tr><tr><td>15-17</td>&#10;			<td>3</td>&#10;		</tr><tr><td>12-14</td>&#10;			<td>1</td>&#10;		</tr><tr><td>0-11</td>&#10;			<td>0</td>&#10;		</tr></tbody></table>

\* Players with 18+ Match Points on Day Two receive an invitation to Pro Tour *Dragons of Tarkir.*
